# Merged-roadmap work on the prerequisite chains of the 100 Annals definitions

Specified in a merged roadmap (`TauCetiRoadmap/<Name>/` at roadmap commit 0e620b7de, 2026-11-25), not
yet (fully) implemented in Tau Ceti main (b22b77af44c, 2026-11-25), and on the prerequisite chain of at
least one definition. Each item was checked against the README section, STATUS.md (snapshot date
given per roadmap; several are stale) and a grep of Tau Ceti code. Item format: status
(NOT-STARTED / PARTIAL with what exists), ready now (yes / no: needs X), definitions served,
evidence. Section and layer ids are the roadmap's own.

## Key ordering facts across items

- SchemeAndStackFoundations §4.14 (Hilbert/Quot) is the choke point for the Jacobian cluster
  (defs 19, 24, 30, 71, 80, 84, 85). Required order: JacobianChallenge §C.1 coherence (finiteness over a
  Noetherian ring is in code for projective schemes and the relative projective case is ready; the proper case then follows by Chow's lemma over an affine base, which
  is in code) and §C.2 base change, then SSF §4.14, then §D.3 Hilbert quotients and §D.4 projective
  Picard representability, then §D.5 `Pic⁰` and the Abel maps and §D.8 the Jacobian. §D.2
  (`HasPicardScheme`, `picardScheme`) is in code. `Symᵈ X` (§C.4) needs only §C.2, not §4.14; §D.5
  needs it.
- SSF Layer 1 is a linear chain: §1.3 (done), then §1.5, §1.6, §1.7/§1.8, §1.9, then §1.19, §1.20,
  §1.21, §1.22, §1.23. §1.5 is partial (`EtaleEquivRel` with `restrict` and `ofAtlas` and the quotient sheaf
  `quotientSheaf` in code), so §1.6 is ready now. §1.18 has `StackInGroupoids`,
  `ofSheaf`/`ofScheme`, `yonedaEquiv`, the generic 2-fibre product, products and the terminal stack (`Stack.pi`, `Stack.terminal`) and a stackification pseudofunctor in code; the
  stack property and universality of stackification are ready now. §1.19's `IsRepresentableBySpaces`
  is in code.
- LieGroups Layer 7 (compact real form, Cartan involution θ) unblocks Layer 9 beyond `GL n`. Layer 9
  in turn heads the real-reductive chain: ArithmeticLocallySymmetricSpaces ALS.F.1 and ALS.0.1–ALS.0.3,
  then AutomorphicFormsOnReductiveGroups §2.3a and §§2.3–2.4, then RealSemisimpleLieGroups Layers 2–5
  and §5.7, for defs 1, 4, 7, 15, 31, 54, 62, 79. Layer 7 is partial (`serreCompactInvolution`, the
  conjugate-linear Chevalley involution of a Serre presentation) and ready now. The Cartan-involution
  predicates are in code at the Lie-algebra level (`LieEquiv.IsCartanInvolution`, with the Cartan
  decomposition) and for real affine groups (`TauCeti.IsCartanInvolution`, ALS.0.1); existence and
  conjugacy of Cartan involutions are not.
- EtaleCohomologyFoundations §F.0.1 (étale module sheaves) precedes every étale cohomology item
  (§F.1, §F.2.x, §F.4.x). It is partial (geometric stalks, finite lisse sheaves and the monoidal
  structure with monoidal stalks in code), and its constructible part is ready now.
- RiemannianGeometry R5a (B, S, normal connection) is formally blocked on SmoothBundlesConnections
  B3–B4 per its README, though that dependency looks soft: the normal bundle `ν_f` with its
  projection, the derivative along a map `CovariantDerivative.alongMap` and the intrinsic B, S and
  ∇⊥ with the Gauss and Weingarten decompositions are in code. It heads the
  chain R5a, then R5a.2 (with R5a.1, whose compact-source case is in code), then R5b (whose operator part,
  `IsCoercive.formOperator`, is in code), then R5c, then MinimalSurfaces MS6, for defs 49, 52, 65,
  66. Varifolds §5.1 and RiemannianManifoldsWithConvexBoundary §3.6 also consume R5a.
- The boundary tangent space, the outward and inward unit normals and boundary defining functions
  (RiemannianManifoldsWithConvexBoundary §§1.1–1.2 and §1.3 except the comparison of defining
  functions; `outwardUnitNormal`, `inwardUnitNormal`, `IsBoundaryDefiningFunctionOn`) and §1.4's pointwise
  `II` (`boundarySecondFundamentalForm`) with its defining-function and normal-extension descriptions are in code, so the rest of §1.3, the rest of §1.4 and
  RiemannianGeometry R7.9 are ready (defs 49, 52, 66). DifferentialGeometry 12.4 still waits on
  `integralDensity` (open PR #38427).
- SchemeAndStackFoundations §§1.19–1.20 (`IsAlgebraicStack`, points of a stack) head all of
  StackPointsAndResidualGerbes (defs 16, 17, 28, 29), none of whose items can start before them;
  they sit at the end of the SSF Layer 1 chain above.
- VirtualFundamentalClass (defs 16, 27) starts at Layer 0 (stack dimension), which needs SSF Layer 1
  through §1.19 (DM stacks) and §1.21 (quotient stacks); its Layer 1 also needs SSF §§1.10, 1.22 and
  §5.5 (partial: the normal cone of an immersion is in code), and its Layer 3 the cotangent-complex
  chain DerivedModuleAlgebra §0.4, then DerivedDeRhamCohomology §5.8. No item can start now.
- AlgebraicModuliForArithmeticGeometry §1.3 `coherentGrassmannian` (partial: the glued relative
  Grassmannian `Scheme.Modules.grassmannian` over any base is in code; its universal quotient and
  universal property are ready) blocks NashBlowups §0.4 and §§1.2–1.5, hence Layers 2, 6 and 7
  (defs 25, 26). NashBlowups §§0.1–0.3 are in code apart from the §§0.1–0.2 Checks; §0.5 is in code apart
  from its cusp Check, and §1.1 is in code; that Check and the rest of §0.6 and of §0.7 can start now.
- LieGroups Layer 9 (`CartanInvolution`, `IwasawaData` beyond `GL n`) also blocks LieGroupsPartII
  Layers 1–3 and §§7.1, 8.1 (defs 1, 4, 54, 79).
- The modular-curve cluster (defs 69, 71, 77) runs through ModularCurvesPartII, whose items wait on
  ModularCurves Layer 10 (untouched), JacobianChallengePartII Layers 0–1 (on JacobianChallenge
  Layers D–E) and EtaleCohomologyFoundations §§F.1–F.4. Only ModularCurvesPartII §3.8 (partial) can
  start now. CompletedCohomologyPartII and CompletedCohomologyAndLocalGlobalCompatibility §§1.1–1.2
  (def 77) wait on the same ModularCurvesPartII §§14.1–14.4a and on ArithmeticLocallySymmetricSpaces
  §§0.1–0.7.
- ShimuraVarieties §§P.1–P.5 (Hodge structures as representations of the Deligne torus) head both
  Mumford–Tate specifications, ShimuraVarieties §P.3a and ShimuraData Layer 1 (defs 13, 23, 89).
  The direction from Hodge structures to Deligne-torus actions on points, the Hodge decomposition of
  a real `S`-comodule (`hodgeComponent`) and the Mumford–Tate Hopf ideal of an algebraic `h`
  (`mumfordTateHopfIdeal`) are in code; the passage from a Hodge structure
  to an algebraic `h` (§P.3) is ready and is the step that makes the construction apply to #13.
- BallQuotientsAndComplexHyperbolicLattices (defs 15, 31) starts from RealSemisimpleLieGroups §5.7,
  hence from LieGroups Layer 9, and its quotients need ComplexComparisonPartII §C-1.1.
- ModularCharactersAndBlocks §0.2 (block idempotents) is in code and so is §6.1 (relative traces,
  the Brauer homomorphism and its kernel) apart from its Checks; with §1.3 (ready, open PR) they head the chain to blocks, defect groups and heights
  (defs 2, 3, 9), and ModularCharactersAndBlocksPartII Layer 6 builds heights on them. Its Layers 1–5
  (Glauberman correspondence, def 8) need only Mathlib and Tau Ceti character theory at the start.
- The CM chain (defs 70, 74, 75) starts from ShimuraVarieties §5.1 `CMType` (ready, open PRs), then
  ComplexMultiplicationAndExplicitReciprocity §1.2 (whose algebraic splitting part can start now),
  then AutomorphicPadicLFunctionsPartII §§0.1, 0.2 and 5.1; the abelian-scheme parts also need
  AbelianSchemesAndArithmeticModuli Layers A1–A5.
- QuasiregularMaps (defs 41, 42) starts with §1.1 (`LinearMap.minimalStretch` and its Checks in code) and §§1.2–1.3 (partial: the outer and inner distortions are in code; ready); its §§2.1–2.3
  also need SobolevMapsBetweenManifolds §§4.2–4.3, §5.1 and §§6.2–6.3 (def 47).
- The Néron–Tate height (def 80) is HeightsRationalPointsAndObstructions §0.19 (§§0.1–0.8 are in code as
  `TauCeti.HeightClass`, and §0.18's Tate limit as `TauCeti.tateLimit`), which needs AbelianSchemesAndArithmeticModuli A1.4–A1.5.
- ReductiveGroups §§9B.5–9B.8 (def 11) define the classical group schemes, which can start now on
  `TauCeti.ConstantForm` and the Clifford API; their comparisons with pinned models wait on
  Layer 9's uniform pinned construction and §§9B.1–9B.3, and the Dickson invariant and smoothness
  on LefschetzPencilsAndVanishingCycles §§2.1–2.2 and DeformationAndDerivedPatchingAlgebra §3.3.
- The entropy chain (defs 96, 97) runs from GeometryOfNumbersAndQuadraticArithmetic §4.2
  (`measureEntropy`, on ProbabilisticAndMetricNumberTheory §5.1a's finite-label entropy) and
  ArithmeticDynamics §§2.6–2.7 to ClassicalErgodicTheory Layer 4, §§6.1–6.2, Layer 7 and §§8.1–8.2;
  none of it is in code. Topological pressure (ClassicalErgodicTheory §§5.2, 5.4–5.5) starts from
  Mathlib's `Dynamics.coverEntropy`, and §5.2 is ready now.

### §1.2 fppf descent of quasi-finite morphisms and of algebraic spaces
- Status: NOT-STARTED
- Ready now: no. `quasiFiniteDescent` (Stacks 02W8) needs effective fpqc descent of quasi-affine morphisms (Stacks 0247; Mathlib has only `Scheme.IsQuasiAffine`, Tau Ceti has effective descent of affine morphisms in Descent/AffineMorphism.lean) and gluing over stable quasi-compact opens; Mathlib's Zariski's Main Theorem supplies quasi-finite separated implies quasi-affine. `spaceFppfDescent` (Stacks 04SK) and 0ADV need §1.17 `artinBootstrap` (not started). The README's *Needs* line lists only §1.3.
- Serves defs: 20 ("fppf descent for alg spaces")
- Evidence: README l.2819 "### 1.2 Fppf descent of quasi-finite morphisms and of algebraic spaces" (Needs §1.3; Mathlib `Presheaf.IsSheaf`, `MorphismProperty.presheaf`, `LocallyQuasiFinite`); no matching declarations in Tau Ceti.

### §1.5 étale equivalence relations and the quotient sheaf
- Status: PARTIAL: `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.Spaces.EtaleEquivRel s t` (Mathlib's internal `IsEquivalenceRelation t s` with `s`, `t` étale) with `refl`, `symm`, `trans` and their laws, `swap`, `hom_ext`, `etale_refl`, `etale_trans`, `id`, `of_isKernelPair` and `EtaleEquivRel.restrict` (the restriction `R ×_{U × U} (U' × U')` along an étale `U' ⟶ U`) (TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry/AlgebraicSpace/EtaleEquivRel.lean), on `TauCeti.CategoryTheory.isEquivalenceRelation_iff` (jointly monic plus an equivalence relation on `T`-points, the README's `IsEquivRel`), `IsKernelPair.isEquivalenceRelation` and `IsEquivalenceRelation.id` (TauCeti/CategoryTheory/EquivalenceRelation.lean). `EtaleEquivRel.ofAtlas` is in code as `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.Spaces.EtaleEquivRel.of_etaleAtlas`: the kernel pair `U ×_F U` of an étale atlas of a presheaf on schemes is an étale equivalence relation (AlgebraicSpace/AtlasRelation.lean), through `CategoryTheory.Functor.relativelyRepresentable.isEquivalenceRelation` for a fully faithful functor (CategoryTheory/MorphismProperty/Representable.lean). The quotient sheaf of a pre-relation of schemes: `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.Spaces.quotientSheaf s t`, the fppf case of the site-level `TauCeti.CategoryTheory.quotientSheaf J s t` with `quotientSheaf.π`, `.desc`, `.isColimit`, `epi_π`, `isLocallySurjective_π` and `.kernelPair` for an equivalence relation, on the presheaf quotient `quotientPresheaf` (AlgebraicSpace/QuotientSheaf.lean, CategoryTheory/Sites/QuotientSheaf.lean, CategoryTheory/QuotientPresheaf.lean). Missing: `quotientSheaf.represented_of`, `quotientSheaf.restrict`. The Checks on the relation itself are in code: `EtaleEquivRel.coprodDesc` with `not_mono_coprodDesc`, `EtaleEquivRel.coprodDesc_gmToAffineLine` (`Δ ⊔ Γ` on `𝔸¹_k`, `2` a unit) with non-isomorphism projections, `EtaleEquivRel.kernelPair_codiag`, and the non-étale and non-monic examples (AlgebraicSpace/EtaleEquivRel/Examples.lean); the quotient-sheaf Checks remain; open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4716 covers `U/Δ ≅ h_U` (`quotientSheaf.isIso_π_self`, `diagonalQuotientSheafIso`, CategoryTheory/Sites/QuotientSheaf.lean, Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/AlgebraicSpace/QuotientSheaf.lean). Open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4733 (`quotientSheaf.represented_of` on any subcanonical site: `quotientSheaf.isIso_desc_of_isLocallySurjective`, `quotientSheaf.isoOfIsLocallySurjective`, CategoryTheory/Sites/QuotientSheaf/Represented.lean).
- Ready now: yes (needs §1.3 only) for `represented_of`, `restrict` and the quotient-sheaf Checks
- Serves defs: 16, 20, 28 (as the start of the §1.6–§1.9 chain)
- Evidence: README l.2917 "### 1.5 Étale equivalence relations and the quotient sheaf"; grep `represented_of` in Tau Ceti: no hits.

- Status: PARTIAL: `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.StackInGroupoids` (a `SchPseudofunctor` with groupoid fibres that is a stack for `Scheme.fppfTopology`), `StackInGroupoids.Hom` (strong transformations), `Hom.IsFiberwiseEquiv` with identities, composition and two-out-of-three, and `StackInGroupoids.ofSheaf` (TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry/Stack/StackInGroupoids.lean) with `ofSheaf.objEquiv`, `ofSheafMap` and `ofScheme` (Stack/OfSheaf.lean), on `Pseudofunctor.ofPresheaf` with `isStack_ofPresheaf_iff` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Discrete.lean); `isFiberedInGroupoids`: `CategoryTheory.Functor.IsFiberedInGroupoids` with `isFiberedInGroupoids_iff` (CategoryTheory/FiberedCategory/FiberedInGroupoids.lean), `Pseudofunctor.CoGrothendieck.isFiberedInGroupoids_forget` (CategoryTheory/FiberedCategory/Grothendieck.lean) and `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.StackInGroupoids.isFiberedInGroupoids` (AlgebraicGeometry/Stack/StackInGroupoids.lean). The generic 2-fibre product: `TauCeti.Stack.twoFiberProduct α β` (objectwise `CategoricalPullback`) with `.fst`, `.snd`, `.iso`, `.lift`, `.homMk`, `.hom_ext` (CategoryTheory/Bicategory/Functor/TwoFiberProduct.lean) and `twoFiberProduct.isStack`, `.isPrestack` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/TwoFiberProduct.lean). The `.ofSheaf` clause: the 2-fibre product of discrete pseudofunctors of presheaves of types is the discrete pseudofunctor of their fibre product, `TauCeti.Stack.twoFiberProduct.isEquivalence_lift_ofPresheafMap_app` and `isPullback_iff_isEquivalence_lift_ofPresheafMap_app` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Discrete/TwoFiberProduct.lean), with the fppf form `StackInGroupoids.isPullback_iff_isEquivalence_lift_ofSheafMap_app` and `isEquivalence_lift_ofSheafMap_yoneda_map_app` for schemes (Stack/OfSheaf.lean). A site-level stackification construction for prestacks: `TauCeti.Stack.stackification`, the pseudofunctor of descent data along `w`-small covering families, with the fully faithful `Stackification.Obj.toObjFunctor` from `F` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Stackification/Basic.lean, Pseudofunctor.lean, Unit.lean); its files state that neither the stack property nor the identification with the stackification is proved. The 2-Yoneda lemma `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.StackInGroupoids.yonedaEquiv` (`Hom(h_T, 𝒳) ≌ 𝒳(T)`, AlgebraicGeometry/Stack/Yoneda.lean), on the site-level `Pseudofunctor.yonedaPullback`, `yonedaEvaluation` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Yoneda.lean). `stackificationDiscreteEquiv` for a separated presheaf `P`: descent data for `ofPresheaf P` form a thin category whose isomorphism classes are Mathlib's `J.plusObj P`, `TauCeti.Stack.Stackification.Obj.isoClassesEquivPlusObj` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Stackification/Discrete.lean). Toward `limit`: products of pseudofunctors `TauCeti.Stack.pi F` with projections `pi.π`, the 2-universal property `pi.lift`, `pi.homMk`, `pi.isoMk`, `pi.hom_ext`, and the terminal pseudofunctor `TauCeti.Stack.terminal` (CategoryTheory/Bicategory/Functor/Pi.lean), with `pi.descentDataEquiv`, `pi.isPrestack`, `pi.isStack` and `terminal.isStack` (CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Pi.lean). Missing: `limit` in general (products, 2-fibre products and the terminal object do not give inserters) and its packaging for the fppf `StackInGroupoids` carrier; the stackification of a general (not prestack) pseudofunctor with `Stack.toStackification` as a strong transformation, `Stack.isStack_stackification`, `Stack.stackificationUniversal`, `stackification_of_isStack`, `stackificationDiscreteEquiv` for a non-separated presheaf, `stackification_isom_sheafify`, `stackification_locally_essSurj`. The 2-fibre product as a stack in groupoids: `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.StackInGroupoids.twoFiberProduct f g` (its projections, 2-cell and lift are the site-level `TauCeti.Stack.twoFiberProduct.fst`, `.snd`, `.iso`, `.lift`), with base change of fibrewise equivalences `twoFiberProduct.isFiberwiseEquiv_snd`, `isFiberwiseEquiv_fst` and the identity Check `isFiberwiseEquiv_snd_id` (AlgebraicGeometry/Stack/TwoFiberProduct.lean). Groupoid fibres of the 2-fibre product and base change of fibrewise equivalences: `TauCeti.Stack.twoFiberProduct.isGroupoid_obj`, `isEquivalence_snd_app` (CategoryTheory/Bicategory/Functor/TwoFiberProduct.lean), on `CategoryTheory.Limits.CategoricalPullback.isGroupoid` (CategoryTheory/Limits/Shapes/Pullback/Categorical/Groupoid.lean). ### §2.6 quasi-coherent cohomology (cited for 20)
- Status: PARTIAL: higher direct images of quasi-coherent modules along qcqs morphisms are quasi-coherent (`AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.Hom.isQuasicoherent_higherDirectImage`), with sections over every affine open `V` of an arbitrary base given by `Hⁿ(f⁻¹ V, M)` linearly over `Γ(Y, V)` (`Scheme.Hom.higherDirectImagePresheafSectionsLinearEquiv`, `higherDirectImageSectionsAddEquiv`) and the localization property (`isLocalizedModule_higherDirectImagePresheaf_map_basicOpen`) (Cohomology/HigherDirectImage/Quasicoherent.lean, Presheaf.lean, Scalars.lean); cohomology on qcqs opens and on qcqs schemes commutes with filtered colimits of quasi-coherent modules (`Scheme.Modules.isColimitMapCoconeCohomologyOnFunctor`, `isColimitMapCoconeCohomologyFunctor`, Cohomology/FilteredColimit.lean); `R^n f_*` of a qcqs `f` over any base preserves colimits of filtered essentially small diagrams of quasi-coherent modules (`Scheme.Hom.isColimitMapCoconeHigherDirectImage`, with the naturality `higherDirectImageSectionsAddEquiv_naturality`, Cohomology/HigherDirectImage/FilteredColimit.lean); flat base change over an affine base for qcqs `X` (`TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.bijective_cohomologyBaseChange`, Cohomology/FlatBaseChange.lean); uniform vanishing (QuasiSeparated.lean); `H^q(ℙⁿ_A, O(d))` in every degree, the top degree as the dual of `Sym^j` (`Module.Basis.nonempty_linearEquiv_cohomology_top_twistingSheaf_dual`, Cohomology/ProjectiveSpace/TopDual.lean), with `Hⁿ(ℙⁿ_R, 𝒪(-n-1)) ≅ R` and nonvanishing of `Hⁿ(ℙⁿ_R, 𝒪(m))` for `m ≤ -n-1` (`nonempty_linearEquiv_cohomology_top_twistingSheaf_neg_card`, `nontrivial_cohomology_top_twistingSheaf_of_le_neg_card`, same file), the input to the `⨿ₙ ℙⁿ_k` Check. The `ℙ¹` Checks are in code: `H¹(ℙ¹_R, 𝒪(−2)) ≅ R`, `dim H⁰(ℙ¹_k, 𝒪(1)) = 2` and the flat base change map on `H¹(ℙ¹, 𝒪(−2))` (`cohomologyOneTwistingSheafNegTwoLinearEquiv`, `finrank_cohomology_zero_twistingSheaf_one`, `cohomologyOneTwistingSheafNegTwoBaseChangeLinearEquiv`, Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/Cohomology/ProjectiveLine.lean), and the Check `H¹(𝔸² ∖ {0}, 𝒪) ≠ 0` over any nonzero ring (`PuncturedAffineSpace.nontrivial_cohomology_one_puncturedPlane`, `not_isAffine_puncturedPlane`, Cohomology/PuncturedPlane.lean). The vanishing-implies-affine direction of `serre_affineness_criterion`: `Scheme.isAffine_of_subsingleton_cohomology_one` (Cohomology/AffinenessCriterion.lean). Missing: flat base change of `R^p f_*` over a non-affine base, `ample_serre_vanishing`, `proper_fibreDimension_vanishing`. The first part of the `⨿ₙ ℙⁿ_k` Check: modules prescribed on a disjoint open cover, `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.Modules.disjointGlue` with `restrictDisjointGlueIso`, `isQuasicoherent_disjointGlue`, `cohomologyOnDisjointGlueIso` (Modules/DisjointGlue.lean), and their higher direct images over a cover of the base by disjoint affine opens, `Scheme.Hom.higherDirectImageSectionsDisjointGlueAddEquiv`, `nontrivial_higherDirectImage_disjointGlue` (Cohomology/HigherDirectImage/DisjointGlue.lean); coproducts of morphisms, whose components are pullbacks, with a property local on the target holding for `∐ f` iff for every `f i` (`AlgebraicGeometry.IsZariskiLocalAtTarget.sigmaMap_iff`, `sigmaMap_preimage_opensRange_sigmaι`, `isPullback_sigmaι_sigmaι_sigmaMap`, AlgebraicGeometry/Limits/Sigma.lean); the case `n = 0`: `H⁰(ℙ⁰_R, 𝒪(m)) ≠ 0` for every `m`, `nontrivial_cohomology_zero_twistingSheaf_of_unique` with `basicOpen_ι_eq_top` (Cohomology/ProjectiveSpace/TwistingSheaf.lean); the transport of `O(-n-1)` to `f⁻¹(V n) ≅ ℙⁿ_k` is not in code; open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4694 (the Check itself: `toSigma R : ⨿ ℙⁿ_R ⟶ ⨿ Spec R`, the module `twist R` gluing the `𝒪(-n-1)`, and `nontrivial_higherDirectImage_twist`, Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/Cohomology/DisjointProjectiveSpaces.lean). ### §4.14 (Grassmannian clause) relative Grassmannian Gr(E, d), Plücker embedding
- Status: PARTIAL: the affine case is done. `Module.Grassmannian.scheme R M k` over `Spec R` for any module `M`, with charts and gluing (Grassmannian/Basic.lean), functor of points on affine tests (`schemeHomEquiv`, Points.lean), separated and proper for finite `M` (`isProper_toSpec`, Proper.lean), base change along `Spec R' → Spec R` (`Module.Grassmannian.isPullback_baseChangeHom`, BaseChange.lean), plus ideal/rank/isotropic loci and the universal quotient. Over an arbitrary base: `Scheme.Modules.grassmannian E k`, the Grassmannian of a quasi-coherent module glued from the affine charts `G(k, Γ(E, U))` over the affine opens `U`, with `grassmannianToBase`, the chart maps `grassmannianι` and `isPullback_grassmannianι_fromSpec` (Grassmannian/Relative/Basic.lean), with its global properties, each local on the base through `grassmannianToBase_iff`: separated for every quasi-coherent `E`, proper for finite type `E`, locally of finite presentation for finitely presented `E`, an isomorphism for `k = 0` (`isProper_grassmannianToBase`, `isIso_grassmannianToBase_zero`, Grassmannian/Relative/Properties.lean), on base change of affine Grassmannians along `IsBaseChange` (`IsBaseChange.grassmannianSchemeHom`, `IsBaseChange.isPullback_grassmannianSchemeHom`, Grassmannian/BaseChange.lean), and the isomorphism `G(k, M) ≅ G(k, M')` induced by a linear equivalence, `Module.Grassmannian.schemeIsoOfLinearEquiv` (Grassmannian/Congr.lean). The Plücker morphism over `Spec R`: `Module.Grassmannian.pluckerHom R M k : scheme R M k ⟶ scheme R (⋀[R]^k M) 1`, characterized on points (`toSchemeHom_comp_pluckerHom`, `pluckerHom_toSpec`, AlgebraicGeometry/Grassmannian/Plucker.lean, RingTheory/Grassmannian/Plucker.lean), alongside the linear-algebra and topological Plücker maps (`LinearAlgebra/ExteriorPower/Plucker.lean`, `LinearAlgebra/Projectivization/Plucker.lean`, `Topology/Grassmannian/Plucker.lean`). On the standard charts the Plücker morphism is a closed immersion: `Module.Grassmannian.ChartAlgebra.pluckerChartHom` with `pluckerChartHom_surjective`, `Module.Grassmannian.isClosedImmersion_specMap_pluckerChartHom` (RingTheory/Grassmannian/Chart/Plucker.lean, AlgebraicGeometry/Grassmannian/Plucker.lean). The `𝒪_X^n` Checks: sections of a finite free sheaf over any open, `FiniteLocallyFreeSheaf.freeSectionsLinearEquiv` (VectorBundle/Free/OpenSections.lean), with `isIso_grassmannianToBase_free` and `isEmpty_grassmannian_free_of_lt` (Grassmannian/Relative/Free.lean); the comparison with the affine construction, `AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.Modules.grassmannianTildeIso M k : grassmannian (tilde M) k ≅ Module.Grassmannian.scheme R M k` over `Spec R` (Grassmannian/Relative/Tilde.lean). Missing: representability on all `S`-schemes (the universal property of the glued Grassmannian), the universal quotient over a non-affine base, the global Plücker closed immersion with `det Q` very ample.

The characters of `W_ℂ = ℂˣ` are in code: `TauCeti.complexUnitsCharacter s k` (`z ↦ |z|^s (z/|z|)^k`, with `z^a zbar^b` as `coe_complexUnitsCharacter_intCast`), the classification `exists_eq_complexUnitsCharacter` and `complexUnitsCharacterEquiv`, and the pullback of `|x|^s sgn(x)^ε` along `z ↦ |z|²`, `realUnitsCharacter_map_normSq` (Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Complex/ArchimedeanCharacter.lean); the one-dimensional parameters of `W_ℝ`: the continuous characters `WeilGroupReal.character s ε` with the classification `characterEquiv`, `exists_eq_character`, through the norm `WeilGroupReal.normHom` and `compNormHomEquiv` (NumberTheory/ClassFieldTheory/WeilGroup/Archimedean/Character.lean, Basic.lean). The `W_ℝ` dimension bound of `irreducibleClassification` is in code: `WeilGroupReal.finrank_le_two_of_isIrreducible` (WeilGroup/Archimedean/IrreducibleDimension.lean), from `Representation.IsIrreducible.finrank_le_index` (RepresentationTheory/Irreducible/FiniteIndex.lean). ### §7.1 Compact real form of a complex semisimple Lie algebra; Cartan involution θ of a real semisimple 𝔤₀
- Status: PARTIAL. Exists: the conjugate-linear involution τ of the Serre-presented algebra, `TauCeti.serreCompactInvolution` (τHᵢ = −Hᵢ, τEᵢ = −Fᵢ, τFᵢ = −Eᵢ, `serreCompactInvolution_lie`, `serreCompactInvolution_involutive`, uniqueness `eq_serreCompactInvolution`, agreement with `serreChevalleyInvolution` for a trivial star; Algebra/Lie/Presentation/Serre/CompactInvolution.lean), on the conjugate Lie algebra `TauCeti.ConjLieAlgebra` (Algebra/Lie/Conjugate.lean). Other ingredients: `serreLiftEquiv` (Serre/Injective.lean), `serreChevalleyInvolution` (Serre/Automorphism.lean), Chevalley involution and structure-constant normalization (Algebra/Lie/Weights/Chevalley/Involution.lean, Algebra/Lie/Weights/StructureConstant/Normalization.lean), and the Lie-algebra Cartan involution predicate `LieEquiv.IsCartanInvolution` with `cartanP` (Algebra/Lie/CartanInvolution/). Missing: the Hermitian form H_τ(x, y) = −B(x, τy) and its positivity, the compact real form 𝔲 = Fix τ, real-form descent, and θ = στ|𝔤₀ (grep `compactRealForm|CompactRealForm` empty). Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/43614 (Algebra/Lie/CompactRealForm/: compact real forms of complex semisimple Lie algebras).
- Ready now: yes. This is a purely Lie-algebraic section. Its inputs (LieHighestWeight Layer 7 Serre presentation and Layer 0 sl₂ strings, and τ) are in place. The later group-level Layer 7 sections (§7.3 universal complexification, §7.4 compact integration) are not ready: they need Layer 4 integration (partial) and Layer 5 covers.

### Layer 9 Cartan, Iwasawa, KAK decompositions (CartanInvolution, IsRealReductive, cartan_polar_decomposition, IwasawaData, restricted roots, kakDecomposition)
- Status: PARTIAL. Only matrix cases exist: Iwasawa `iwasawaDiffeomorph` (Geometry/Lie/Iwasawa.lean, LinearAlgebra/Matrix/GeneralLinearGroup/Iwasawa.lean), KAK for GL_n(ℝ/ℂ) (LinearAlgebra/Matrix/GeneralLinearGroup/Cartan.lean), maximal compact (…/MaximalCompact.lean), symplectic polar and Cartan involution (Analysis/Matrix/Polar/Symplectic.lean, Geometry/Lie/Symplectic/Cartan.lean), the polar decomposition of the realified `GL(n, ℂ)` and its maximal compact subgroups (realified unitary group maximal compact, every compact subgroup conjugate into it; Analysis/Matrix/Polar/Realify.lean, LinearAlgebra/Matrix/GeneralLinearGroup/Realify/MaximalCompact.lean), unitary upper-triangular with positive diagonal = 1 (Analysis/Matrix/Triangular.lean); the Cartan decomposition `Subgroup.cartanHomeomorph` of a star-stable subgroup of the units of a C*-algebra (Analysis/SpecialFunctions/ContinuousFunctionalCalculus/Cartan.lean), instantiated for `SL n`, `Sp(2n, ℝ)` and the realified `GL(n, ℂ)` (Analysis/Matrix/Cartan.lean, Analysis/Matrix/Cartan/Realify.lean), with the polar order `A = exp X * realify U` on `Sp(2n, ℝ)` as `TauCeti.GLSymplectic.leftCartanHomeomorph` (Analysis/Matrix/Cartan/Realify.lean); the positive upper-triangular group `A N ⊂ GL_n(𝕜)` as a Lie group with global exponential coordinates, the first half of the "Connected subgroups of the positive triangular group" bullet (`TauCeti.Lie.posUpperTriangularLieGroup`, the inverse `posUpperTriangularLog`, the diffeomorphism `posUpperTriangularExpDiffeomorph`, `lieExp_posUpperTriangular_bijective`, the comparison with the matrix exponential `coe_lieExp_posUpperTriangular`, the range of the differential `mem_range_posUpperTriangularLieAlgebraMap_iff`, Geometry/Lie/Subgroup/PosUpperTriangular/{Basic,Exponential}.lean), and its Lie algebra of upper-triangular matrices with real diagonal as a real completely solvable Lie algebra (`TauCeti.upperTriangularRealDiagonalLieSubalgebra`, `isSolvable_upperTriangularRealDiagonalLieSubalgebra`, `splits_charpoly_ad_upperTriangularRealDiagonalLieSubalgebra`, `im_eq_zero_of_hasEigenvalue_baseChange_ad_upperTriangularRealDiagonalLieSubalgebra`, Algebra/Lie/GeneralLinear/RealTriangular.lean). Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45233 (the global matrix model: for a closed subgroup `H ≤ GL(n, 𝕜)`, `𝕜 = ℝ` or `ℂ`, stable under conjugate transpose with finitely many components, polar factors lie in `H`, `K_H × 𝔭_H ≃ₜ H` and `K_H` is maximal compact, `Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.cartan_polar_decomposition`, with Mostow's lemma, Analysis/Matrix/Cartan/Closed.lean, Analysis/Matrix/Exponential/Mostow.lean). The §9.3 general-linear form is in code: for an injective immersive homomorphism `f : C →* GL_n(𝕜)` of a connected real Lie group with values in `A N`, `TauCeti.Lie.lieExp_bijective_of_immersed_posUpperTriangular`, `lieExpDiffeomorphOfImmersedPosUpperTriangular`, `isSmoothEmbedding_of_immersed_posUpperTriangular`, `isClosedEmbedding_of_immersed_posUpperTriangular` (Geometry/Lie/Subgroup/PosUpperTriangular/GeneralLinear.lean); `K ∩ C = {1}` for immersed `C` is not stated separately, and the Iwasawa export equations of §9.3 are not in code.

- Status: PARTIAL. ### Layer 5 (solvable groups) Structure of smooth connected solvable groups, G = G_u ⋊ T
- Status: PARTIAL. Exists: `torus_coinvariants_unipotentRadical` (G/G_u is a torus), and the triviality of homomorphisms from diagonalizable groups to unipotent groups, scheme-theoretically, so a diagonalizable closed subgroup of a unipotent group is trivial (`TauCeti.geometricallyUnipotentPointsCommHopfAlgProperty.bialgHom_eq_unit_comp_counit_of_groupLikeSetSpan_eq_top`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Unipotent/Diagonalizable.lean, on `Module.Basis.map_coefficientMatrix_eq_one_of_isUpperTriangular_of_groupLikeSetSpan_eq_top`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Representation/UpperUnitriangular.lean); separable base change and descent of the unipotent radical; a diagonalizable closed subgroup or torus meets `R_u(G)` trivially and maps with trivial kernel to `G ⧸ R_u(G)` (`sup_unipotentRadicalDefiningIdeal_eq_augmentation_of_torus`, `kernelHopfIdeal_coinvariantsι_unipotentRadical_comp_mkQuotient_eq_augmentation_of_torus`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Unipotent/Radical/Diagonalizable/Basic.lean). Split extensions of affine groups are semidirect products: if a closed subgroup `T` maps isomorphically onto `G ⧸ R_u(G)` then `R_u(G) ⋊ T → G` is an isomorphism (`TauCeti.CommHopfAlgCat.isIso_productMapOfNormal_of_isIso`, HopfIdeal/Normal/Product/Split.lean; `FiniteTypeCommHopfAlgCat.isIso_productMapOfNormal_unipotentRadical_of_isIso`, Unipotent/Radical/SemidirectProduct.lean), which reduces `G = G_u ⋊ T` to the isomorphism of a maximal torus onto `G ⧸ G_u`. A homomorphism of finite-type affine groups with geometrically reduced source and trivial kernel is a closed immersion, and an isomorphism of smooth connected groups when it does not drop the Lie dimension (Milne 5.34), `TauCeti.CommHopfAlgCat.surjective_iff_kernelHopfIdeal_eq_augmentation_of_isGeometricallyReduced`, `isIso_of_kernelHopfIdeal_eq_augmentation_of_finrank_le` (Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/HopfIdeal/Quotient/Kernel/ClosedImmersion.lean), applied to a closed subgroup `T` meeting a normal subgroup such as `R_u(G)` trivially, `TauCeti.FiniteTypeCommHopfAlgCat.isIso_coinvariantsι_comp_mkQuotient` (HopfIdeal/Coinvariants/ClosedImmersion.lean); for a maximal torus this leaves the dimension count `dim T ≥ dim G/G_u`. Over a perfect field (Milne 16.33(a)–(c)), `G ⧸ R_u(G)` is a torus (`FiniteTypeCommHopfAlgCat.torus_coinvariants_unipotentRadical_of_perfectField`, Solvable/UnipotentRadical/Basic.lean) and the unipotent radical commutes with every field extension (`FiniteTypeCommHopfAlgCat.baseChangeHopfIdeal_unipotentRadicalDefiningIdeal_of_perfectField`, Solvable/UnipotentRadical/PerfectField.lean, with `unipotentRadicalDefiningIdeal_eq_of_multiplicativeType_coinvariants`, Unipotent/Radical/Torus/Quotient.lean). Over an algebraically closed field, a torus `T` of a connected reduced `G` with `dim Lie(G ⧸ R_u(G)) ≤ dim Lie(T)` maps isomorphically onto `G ⧸ R_u(G)` and `R_u(G) ⋊ T → G` is an isomorphism: `FiniteTypeCommHopfAlgCat.isIso_coinvariantsι_unipotentRadical_comp_mkQuotient_of_torus`, `isIso_productMapOfNormal_unipotentRadical_of_torus` (Unipotent/Radical/Torus/Complement.lean). Missing: the existence of a torus of dimension `dim(G/G_u)` in a connected solvable group (so that `G = G_u ⋊ T` holds for maximal tori), and conjugacy of subgroups of multiplicative type into T by G_u(k̄) (Milne, Theorem 16.33(b),(d)). STATUS lists Layer 5 as done. Open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4749 (Layer 5's comodule definition of unipotence, `TauCeti.unipotentCommHopfAlgProperty` with `unipotentCommHopfAlgProperty_iff`, `hasNonzeroFixedVector`, `exists_basis_coefficientMatrix_isUpperUnitriangular` and the unipotence Checks, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Unipotent/Comodule.lean).
- Ready now: yes. It is the input that Layer 7 torus conjugacy, and through it the root datum, wait on.
- Serves defs: 1, 4, 11, 23, 79
- Evidence: README l.2368 "## Layer 5: solvable and unipotent groups; the unipotent radical", l.2393 "**Solvable groups.**" bullet ("Prove, over `k̄`, the decomposition `G = G_u ⋊ T` ... (Milne, Theorem 16.33(d), p. 336)"); grep finds no `G_u ⋊ T` decomposition in Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/Solvable/ or Unipotent/; Layer 7 torus conjugacy goes through Borel containment (Borel/Conjugacy/Containment.lean) and then conjugacy of maximal tori inside the solvable Borel.

The normal collar of a compact hypersurface with a continuous unit normal field `ν` is in code as an open embedding of `M × (-ε, ε)` and, reparametrized, a bicollar: `TauCeti.SmoothEmbedding.exists_isOpenEmbedding_normalCollar`, `exists_isBicollar_normalCollar` (Geometry/Manifold/TubularNeighborhood/Riemannian/NormalCollar/Basic.lean), with `TauCeti.VectorBundle.unitSectionHomeomorph` (Topology/VectorBundle/UnitSection.lean), and smooth: a `PartialDiffeomorph` `M × ℝ → N` with source `M × (-ε, ε)` equal there to `(x, t) ↦ exp_{f x}(t ν x)`, `TauCeti.SmoothEmbedding.exists_partialDiffeomorph_normalCollar` (TubularNeighborhood/Riemannian/NormalCollar/Smooth.lean), on `TauCeti.VectorBundle.unitSectionDiffeomorph` (Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/UnitSection.lean); the two sides `U±` and the smooth collar coordinate are in code: `TauCeti.NormalCollar.sidePos`, `sideNeg`, `coordinate` with `target_eq_union`, `isOpen_sidePos`, `isPreconnected_sidePos`, `mem_sidePos_iff`, `contMDiffOn_coordinate` (TubularNeighborhood/Riemannian/NormalCollar/{Sides,Smooth}.lean). The separation theorem (1) for a bicollared hypersurface with closed image in a connected manifold with `H¹_dR(M) = 0` is in code, `TauCeti.IsBicollar.separates_of_subsingleton_deRhamCohomology_one` (Geometry/Manifold/DeRham/Separation.lean). The two-sidedness predicate `TauCeti.IsTwoSidedHypersurface` with `isTwoSidedHypersurface_iff_nonempty_vectorBundleOrientation` and the unit normal double cover `S(ν_f)` as a two-sheeted covering, `isCoveringMap_sphereBundleProj_orthogonalNormalSpace`, are in code (Geometry/Manifold/Riemannian/Immersion/TwoSided.lean); the crossing form `ω_ν` is not.

### §F.0.2 Finite étale Galois categories, `EtaleFundamentalGroup` and the étale fundamental groupoid
- Status: PARTIAL, and enough for def 84's use of π₁. In code: `PreGaloisCategory (FiniteEtaleScheme S)` for every S (AlgebraicGeometry/FiniteEtale/Quotient.lean); `FiberFunctor (fiber x)` and `GaloisCategory (FiniteEtaleScheme S)` for connected S, with any two geometric points joined by a path (FiniteEtale/Galois/Category.lean); the étale fundamental group `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.EtaleFundamentalGroup x = Aut (fiber x)` and étale paths `EtalePath x y = (fiber x ≅ fiber y)` (FiniteEtale/Fiber.lean), a torsor under `Aut` when nonempty (`CategoryTheory.Aut.instTorsorIso`, CategoryTheory/Aut/Torsor.lean), with functoriality `mapAutFiber`/`mapFiberIso` and change of basepoint `mapAutFiber_conjAut` (Galois/Functoriality.lean); paths on disconnected bases (Galois/Component.lean); connectedness criteria for finite étale schemes (Galois/Connected.lean); `galContinuousMulEquivAutFiber : Gal(Ω/K) ≃ₜ* π₁(Spec K,x)` (Galois/FundamentalGroup.lean), under which the map of étale fundamental groups of fields is Galois restriction, `mapAutFiber_galContinuousMulEquivAutFiber` (Galois/FundamentalGroup.lean, with `mapAutFiber_eq_of_fiberCompInclIso_left`, Galois/Functoriality.lean); the Galois action on stalks at geometric points `GeometricPoint.galoisStalkAction` (Sites/Etale/GeometricPoint.lean) and, on a field, the stalk as a continuous `Gal(Ω/K)`-representation `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.galoisStalk` with `isOpen_stabilizer_galoisStalk` (Sites/Etale/GaloisStalk.lean); the first half of the clopen Check, extension of finite étale covers along a finite étale monomorphism, `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.map` with `mapCompPullbackIso`, `mapCompFiberIso` (FiniteEtale/Map.lean); the first half of `VanishingCycles.fieldSheavesEquiv`, étale sheaves on `Spec K` as sheaves on finite étale `K`-schemes and on finite continuous `Gal(Ω/K)`-sets, `FiniteEtaleScheme.fieldSheafEquiv`, `galoisSheafEquiv` (FiniteEtale/Galois/FieldSheaf.lean). The cover side of lisse monodromy: `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.monodromyEquivalence x : FiniteEtaleScheme S ≌ ContAction FintypeCat (Aut (fiber x))` for connected `S`, with `monodromyEquivalenceGrp`, `monodromyEquivalenceCommGrp` (FiniteEtale/Galois/Monodromy.lean) and, for any fundamental group `G` of the fibre functor, `CategoryTheory.PreGaloisCategory.functorToContActionEquivOfIsFundamentalGroup` (CategoryTheory/Galois/Equivalence.lean). Missing: `EtaleFundamentalGroup.map` and `EtaleFundamentalGroup.small`; `lisseMonodromyEquiv` (the identification of locally constant finite étale sheaves with covers and the passage to `Λ`-module sheaves); tensor/dual transport. Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/43299 (small étale restriction and effective étale descent for representability by finite étale schemes, toward lisse monodromy, FiniteEtale/Sheaf/Pullback.lean, FiniteEtale/Descent.lean). Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45387 (toward `EtaleFundamentalGroup.small`: `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.small_aut_fiber` when the finite étale covers form an essentially small category, and `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.essentiallySmall_of_isAffine`, FiniteEtale/Small.lean, CategoryTheory/Functor/LocallySmall.lean; the non-affine base remains). The group-theoretic half for sheaves of sets is in code: `ContAction.sheafEquiv G` between sheaves on finite continuous `G`-sets and `Action.SmoothSet G` for compact `G` (CategoryTheory/Galois/Sheaf.lean), composed with `jointlySurjectiveSheafEquiv` through `FiniteEtaleScheme.galoisTopology_eq` (`galoisTopology K Ω` is the jointly surjective topology) into `TauCeti.VanishingCycles.fieldSheavesEquiv K Ω`, étale sheaves of sets on `Spec K` as `Action.SmoothSet Gal(Ω/K)` (FiniteEtale/Galois/SmoothSheaf.lean); the `Λ`-module version is not in code. The second half of the clopen Check: for a finite étale monomorphism `g` the map of étale fundamental groups is an isomorphism, `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.mapAutFiberEquivOfMono`, and for a clopen `U` of `S`, `mapAutFiber_ι_bijective_of_isClosed` (FiniteEtale/Galois/Clopen.lean), through the adjunction `FiniteEtaleScheme.mapPullbackAdj` (FiniteEtale/Map.lean) and `CategoryTheory.Adjunction.mapAutWhiskeringLeftEquiv` (CategoryTheory/Adjunction/MapAut.lean). The Check on étale paths over `Spec 𝔽_p`: `EtalePath x x` is `Gal(𝔽_p^alg/𝔽_p)` acting simply transitively on both sides, and the fibre functors at `𝔽_p^alg` and at an algebraic closure of `𝔽_p(t)` are isomorphic while the fields are not (Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/FiniteEtale/EtalePath.lean); `Gal ≅ Ẑ` is not in code: open Tau Ceti PR https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4332 (`TauCeti.FiniteField.galoisGroupEquivZHat : Gal(L/k) ≃ₜ* ℤ̂` for a finite field `k`, Frobenius to the generator, and `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.etaleFundamentalGroupFiniteFieldEquivZHat`, FieldTheory/Finite/GaloisZHat.lean, AlgebraicGeometry/FiniteEtale/Galois/FiniteField.lean; the `Spec 𝔽_(p²) → Spec 𝔽_p` index-two Check waits on #43620), on the shared lemma `TauCeti.zHat.mem_zpowers_mk_up_ofAdd_one` (in code, Topology/Algebra/Group/Profinite/ZHat/Basic.lean).
- Ready now: yes: `EtaleFundamentalGroup.map` and `.small` need only what is in code; lisse monodromy can use `CategoryTheory.Sheaf.IsFiniteLisse` and the fully faithful `TauCeti.FiniteEtaleScheme.toSheafFullyFaithful` (STATUS frontier).

### B1.4 Slices, quotients and homogeneous spaces G/H
- Status: PARTIAL: the free proper quotient theorem is in code: for a finite-dimensional real Lie group acting smoothly, freely and properly on a boundaryless manifold, `M ⧸ G` is a manifold of dimension `dim M − dim G` (`ProperSMul.orbitRelQuotientChartedSpace`, `instIsManifoldOrbitRelQuotient`) and the orbit map is a smooth submersion (`isSubmersion_quotientMk`, `ker_mfderiv_quotientMk`), Geometry/Manifold/GroupAction/Quotient.lean, with its inputs (transverse slice charts, GroupAction/{SliceChart,TransverseSlice,Orbit}.lean; compact stabilizers and closed orbits of proper actions; Haar-averaged invariant metrics; smooth functions on orbit spaces). Smooth G/H for a closed subgroup is in code (`Subgroup.quotientChartedSpaceOfIsClosed`, `Subgroup.isManifold_quotientOfIsClosed`, `Subgroup.isSubmersion_quotientMk`, `ker_mfderiv_quotientMk`, the smooth `G`-action `contMDiffSMul_quotientOfIsClosed`, Geometry/Lie/Subgroup/Quotient.lean, with quotients by closed normal subgroups in NormalQuotient.lean), as are balanced products `P ×_K Z` and `G ×_K Z` as manifolds (`TauCeti.BalancedProduct.isManifold`, `isSubmersion_orbitProj`, GroupAction/BalancedProduct.lean, Geometry/Lie/Subgroup/BalancedProduct.lean) and equivariant linear slices at points with compact stabilizer (`TauCeti.exists_equivariant_slice_of_isCompact_stabilizer`, `exists_equivariant_contMDiff_chart_of_isCompact_stabilizer`, GroupAction/EquivariantSlice.lean). The Hopf quotient `S²ⁿ⁺¹ → CPⁿ` is a smooth submersion and the quotient structure is the homogeneous-ratio structure (`TauCeti.ComplexProjectiveSpace.isSubmersion_mk`, `orbitRelQuotientDiffeomorph`, Geometry/Manifold/Instances/ComplexProjectiveSpace/Hopf.lean). The slice theorem at the identity coset: the descended action map `[g, z] ↦ g • ι z` on `G ×_K Z` is a local diffeomorphism at `[1, z₀]` when `dι` is injective onto a complement of `𝔤 · ι z₀` and `dim K = dim 𝔤_{ι z₀}`, `TauCeti.BalancedProduct.isLocalDiffeomorphAt_liftRightTranslation_one`, with the general criterion `isLocalDiffeomorphAt_liftRightTranslation` (Geometry/Manifold/GroupAction/Slice/Theorem.lean). Along whole orbits the descended action map is a local diffeomorphism, `isLocalDiffeomorph_liftRightTranslation` (Slice/Theorem.lean), and the zero section of the slice model at a `K`-fixed point, `TauCeti.BalancedProduct.zeroSectionRightTranslation : G ⧸ K → G ×_K Z`, is a `G`-equivariant smooth embedding onto the orbit of `[1, z₀]` (`isSmoothEmbedding_zeroSectionRightTranslation`, Topology/Covering/BalancedProduct/RightTranslation.lean, Geometry/Lie/Subgroup/BalancedProduct.lean). The principal-bundle clause for free proper actions is in code: the orbit projection is a smooth principal `G`-bundle for `x · g = g⁻¹ • x`, `ProperSMul.principalBundle_orbitRelFiber`, `contMDiffFiberBundle_orbitRelFiber`, `exists_principalBundle_orbitRelFiber`, with the charted total space diffeomorphic to `M` (`ProperSMul.orbitRelFiberDiffeomorph`, Geometry/Manifold/FiberBundle/Principal/Quotient.lean). Missing: the assembled slice theorem `G ×_H B ≅ G • S` (the map `[g, v] ↦ g · exp_x(v)`, shrinking, injectivity near the orbit by properness and the diffeomorphism onto a `G`-invariant neighbourhood).
- Ready now: yes for the slice theorem (it consumes B1.1's `TauCeti.PrincipalBundle` API, the balanced products and the equivariant slices, all in Tau Ceti)

The Lipschitz area formula ℝᵐ→ℝⁿ with multiplicity is in MeasureTheory/Function/AreaFormula/{Basic,Lipschitz,Multiplicity}.lean, and the coarea formula for locally Lipschitz maps with a.e. countably rectifiable fibres is in MeasureTheory/Function/Coarea/Lipschitz.lean and Geometry/MeasureTheory/Rectifiable/Coarea.lean. - Status: PARTIAL, all but §5.4's converse criterion: perimeter API, essential and reduced boundaries, outer normal, density estimates, blow-ups (Analysis/BoundedVariation/BlowUp.lean), countable `(n−1)`-rectifiability of the reduced boundary `countablyRectifiable_reducedBoundary` (Analysis/BoundedVariation/Rectifiable.lean), De Giorgi's theorem `|Dχ_E| = Hⁿ⁻¹⌞∂*E` (`variationMeasure_eq_comap_restrict_reducedBoundary`, `perimeter_eq_euclideanHausdorffMeasure_reducedBoundary`) with Gauss–Green against `Hⁿ⁻¹` on the reduced boundary (Analysis/BoundedVariation/DeGiorgi.lean), the essential/reduced comparison up to an `Hⁿ⁻¹`-null set `essentialBoundary_inter_ae_eq_reducedBoundary` and §5.4's equality `P(s; Ω) = Hⁿ⁻¹(∂ᴹs ∩ Ω)` for `n ≥ 2`, `perimeter_eq_euclideanHausdorffMeasure_essentialBoundary_inter` (Analysis/BoundedVariation/EssentialBoundary.lean), with the essential boundaries of the §5.4 Check sets `essentialBoundary_ball`, `essentialBoundary_ball_sdiff_singleton`, `essentialBoundary_ball_union_rational` (MeasureTheory/Covering/EssentialBoundary/Ball.lean), `Hⁿ⁻¹(∂*s ∩ Ω) ≤ P(s; Ω)` in every dimension (`euclideanHausdorffMeasure_reducedBoundary_inter_le_perimeter`, Analysis/BoundedVariation/HausdorffMeasure.lean), the frontier case of the criterion, `P(s; Ω) ≤ Hⁿ⁻¹(∂s ∩ Ω)` for every set in dimension `n ≥ 2` (`perimeter_le_euclideanHausdorffMeasure_frontier_inter`) and locally finite perimeter from a frontier of locally finite `Hⁿ⁻¹` measure (`exists_perimeter_ne_top_of_euclideanHausdorffMeasure_frontier_inter_ne_top`, Analysis/BoundedVariation/FrontierCriterion.lean), and the one-dimensional criterion `P(s; Ω) ≤ #(∂ᵉs ∩ Ω)` for null-measurable `s ⊆ ℝ`, `TauCeti.Real.perimeter_le_encard_essentialBoundary_inter`, with `TauCeti.Real.perimeter_le_encard_frontier_inter` (Analysis/BoundedVariation/OneDimensional/EssentialBoundary.lean). Bundle sections: the Banach space `TauCeti.HolderSection A k α` of sections of a vector bundle whose chart readings over a `FiniteBundleAtlas` are `C^{k,α}` (`FiniteBundleAtlas.holderSections`, `isClosed_holderSections`, `MemHolderSection`, Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/Holder/{Basic,ContMDiffSection}.lean), and its little Hölder space `TauCeti.HolderSection.littleHolder`, the closure of the smooth sections, complete, with chartwise vanishing small-scale modulus for `α < 1` (`mem_littleHolder_component_of_mem_littleHolder`, Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/Holder/Little/Basic.lean), independent of the atlas (`HolderSection.littleHolder_map_atlasEquiv`, `atlasEquiv_mem_littleHolder_iff`, `smoothSections_map_atlasLinearEquiv`, Holder/Little/Atlas.lean). The bundle comparison under atlas and frame changes: for `k + 1 ≤ n`, `α ≤ 1` on a compact boundaryless manifold, `FiniteBundleAtlas.memHolderSection_iff_of_atlas` and, for a complete model fibre, the Banach isomorphism `HolderSection.atlasEquiv` with `exists_norm_atlasEquiv_le` (Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/Holder/Atlas.lean). The order-lowering inclusion `C^{k+1,α}_A(M, F) → C^{k,β}_A(M, F)` for `β ≤ 1`, `CompactChartAtlas.CkHolderSpace.inclusionSuccL` with `inclusionSuccL_injective` and its compatibilities with `inclusionL` (Chart/Holder/Inclusion.lean). The Euclidean step of the converse little Hölder characterization, support-controlled smooth approximation in `h^{k,α}`, `TauCeti.CkHolderSpace.exists_mem_smoothBoundedDerivatives_tsupport_subset_norm_sub_lt` and `exists_mem_smoothBoundedDerivatives_hasCompactSupport_tsupport_subset_norm_sub_lt` (Analysis/Holder/Little/Basic.lean). The bundle Besov spaces of §5.3, whose case `p = q = ∞` is the Zygmund carrier, are in code for functionals on bundle tests over a fixed square chart partition: `TauCeti.SquareChartPartition.MemBesov`, the complete space `besovSpace` with `isClosed_besovSpace`, the inclusions `besovInclusion`, `besovInclusionOfLT`, independence of the bump `MemBesov.of_bump` and `besovSpaceEquivSobolevSpace` at `p = q = 2` (Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/Sobolev/Besov.lean). - Status: PARTIAL: a generic boundaryless bundle carrier `TauCeti.SobolevSection A μ p k` (`W^{k,p}(V)` over a `FiniteBundleAtlas`, closedness/completeness, `lowerOrderL`) exists in TauCeti/Geometry/Manifold/VectorBundle/Sobolev/{Basic,ContMDiffSection,SMul}.lean, with independence of the atlas over any compact boundaryless base (`SobolevSection.atlasEquiv`, Sobolev/Atlas/{Equiv,Transfer}.lean), density of smooth sections for finite `p` (`SobolevSection.denseRange_ofContMDiffSection`, Sobolev/Density.lean), Morrey's embedding in every order and dimension (`SobolevSection.HasContinuousRepresentatives.of_finrank_lt_mul`, Sobolev/Morrey.lean), Rellich–Kondrachov (`SobolevSection.exists_isCompactOperator_of_inv_lt_inv_add`, `isCompactOperator_lowerOrderL`, Sobolev/Rellich.lean) and chart transfer; for surfaces modelled on `ℂ` its chart readings are the local classes `MemWkpLoc` and conversely (`TauCeti.SobolevSection.memWkpLoc_coord`, Sobolev/Local.lean), in arbitrary bundle charts rather than isothermal ones. For `∂Σ ≠ ∅`, `TauCeti.BorderedSobolevSection` over a half-plane `FiniteBundleAtlas` (FiniteBundleAtlas/HalfPlane.lean) is complete, contains the `Cᵏ` sections and is closed under multiplication by smooth functions (Sobolev/Bordered/{Basic,SMul}.lean); first-order Morrey up to the boundary, `TauCeti.BorderedSobolevSection.toSection` with `existsUnique_continuous_ae_eq_halfCoord` and `exists_holderOnWith_halfCoord_toSection` (Sobolev/Bordered/Morrey.lean); and for `2 < p ≤ ∞` the closed subspace `W^{1,p}_F(V)` of sections with boundary values in a family of fibre subspaces, `TauCeti.BorderedSobolevSection.withBoundaryIn` (Sobolev/Bordered/BoundaryCondition.lean); readings in arbitrary half-plane charts, `BorderedSobolevSection.exists_chartValueL_ae_eq_halfCoord` with `TauCeti.FiniteBundleAtlas.memWkpLoc_halfCoord_extChartAt_target` (Sobolev/Bordered/Atlas/Transfer.lean), and the change of atlas `BorderedSobolevSection.changeAtlas`, `atlasEquiv` with `exists_norm_changeAtlas_le` (Sobolev/Bordered/Atlas/Equiv.lean), whose transfer hypothesis holds between any two finite bundle atlases of a `Cⁿ` bundle, `n ≥ 1`, for `k ≤ n`, `BorderedSobolevSection.transfersTo_of_contMDiffVectorBundle` (Sobolev/Bordered/Atlas/Transfer.lean), so the norm is independent of the atlas; extension by zero from one chart, `BorderedSobolevSection.exists_chartValueL_ae_eq_halfCoord_of_eq_zero` (Sobolev/Bordered/ExtensionByZero.lean, with `FiniteBundleAtlas.focus` and `Wkp.mem_closure_range_restrictL_ofTestFunctionₗ_top_upperHalf`, Analysis/Sobolev/Wkp/HalfPlaneDensity.lean); and the negative half of the closed-disc Rellich Check, `BorderedSobolevSection.not_isCompactOperator_id` with `not_isCompactOperator_id_unitClosedDisc` (Sobolev/Bordered/InfiniteDimensional{,/Disc}.lean). ### Layer 8 Hochschild cochains (ordinary algebras, normalized, HH²)
- Status: PARTIAL. Positive-arity Hochschild cochains of an A∞ algebra, with the Hochschild differential `[m,-]`, braces, the Gerstenhaber bracket and cohomology (Algebra/Homology/AInfinity/Algebra/Hochschild/{Basic,Complex,Cohomology}.lean). Normalized cochains for a strict unit, `PositiveHochschild.normalizedComplex` (Hochschild/Normalized.lean); whether the inclusion is a quasi-isomorphism is not addressed. Formal deformations and obstruction classes in Hochschild degree two (Deformation.lean). An ordinary or DG algebra becomes an A∞ algebra via `IsNonUnitalDGAlgebra.toAInfinityAlgebra` (DG.lean). For an ordinary algebra `A` and an `A`-bimodule `M`, the Hochschild coboundary `TauCeti.hochschildBimoduleCoboundary` (RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/Coboundary.lean) and the cochain complex and cohomology with bimodule coefficients, `TauCeti.hochschildBimoduleCochainComplex`, `hochschildBimoduleCohomology` (RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/Bimodule.lean, Tau Ceti PR #37750); matrix Morita invariance of `HHⁿ`, `TauCeti.hochschildCohomology.matrixExtensionEquiv` (HochschildCohomology/Matrix.lean). HH² classifies infinitesimal deformations of an ordinary algebra: `TauCeti.IsInfinitesimalDeformation` with `isInfinitesimalDeformation_iff_mem_hochschildBimoduleCocycles` and `TauCeti.InfinitesimalDeformation.equivHochschildBimoduleCohomology` (RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/InfinitesimalDeformation.lean). Missing: HH³ ↔ obstructions for ordinary algebras, normalization comparison, and quasi-equivalence/Morita invariance beyond matrix algebras. Open Tau Ceti PR https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/45290 (HH³ obstructions: `TauCeti.IsAlgebraDeformationOfOrder`, the obstruction `TauCeti.algebraDeformationObstruction` is a Hochschild 3-cocycle, and its class `IsAlgebraDeformationOfOrder.obstructionClass` in `HH³(A, A)` vanishes exactly when the deformation extends, RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/DeformationObstruction.lean); with it the ordinary-algebra clause is covered. Normalized Hochschild cochains with bimodule coefficients are in code: `TauCeti.hochschildBimoduleNormalizedCochainComplex`, a subcomplex of `hochschildBimoduleCochainComplex`, with the inclusion bijective on `HH⁰`, `HH¹`, `HH²` at the level of cocycles and coboundaries (`exists_sub_hochschildBimoduleCoboundary_mem_hochschildBimoduleNormalizedCochains_two`, `mem_hochschildBimoduleNormalizedCochains_one_of_hochschildBimoduleCoboundary_mem`, RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/Normalized.lean), and the inclusion is a quasi-isomorphism in degrees `n ≤ 2`, `TauCeti.quasiIsoAt_hochschildBimoduleNormalizedCochainComplexι` (RingTheory/HochschildCohomology/Normalized.lean); the normalization comparison in all degrees and for `A∞` algebras is not.
- Ready now: yes. The README names no blocking prerequisites for the ordinary-algebra HH²/HH³ clause, and both the A∞ machinery it specializes and the bimodule-coefficient cohomology are present.

### G4.2 Principal spin lifts
- Status: PARTIAL: the structure, its equivalences and pullback are in code, on a general carrier for lifts of the orthonormal frame bundle along a homomorphism `ρ : G →* (Fin n → ℝ) ≃ₗ[ℝ] (Fin n → ℝ)`: `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameLift` with `OrthonormalFrameLift.Equiv` (`refl`, `symm`, `trans`), `pullback`, `Equiv.pullback` and extension of lifts along structure-group homomorphisms (Topology/VectorBundle/OrthonormalFrame/Lift/Basic.lean); `TauCeti.SpinStructure` for rank `n ≥ 1` (a lift for `realCliffordSpinGroupZero n` along `CliffordAlgebra.spinVectorAction`, no numerability field, as the README's `Suggested.lean` specifies), with the sheet exchange `OrthonormalFrameLift.Equiv.negOne`, the induced orientation `SpinStructure.orientation` and the lifted frame map into oriented frames `SpinStructure.toOrientedFrames` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/{Basic,Orientation}.lean); `TauCeti.SpinCStructure` on the same carrier, with the Spinᶜ structure of a Spin structure `SpinStructure.toSpinC` and its nowhere-vanishing determinant-line section `determinantLineSection` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/OfSpin.lean, FourManifolds §4.1). Transport along continuous fibrewise isometries and along a change of metric is in code: `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameLift.map` with `Equiv.map`, `equivMapEquiv`, `nonempty_equiv_map_iff` and `pullback_map` (Topology/VectorBundle/OrthonormalFrame/Lift/Basic.lean), `Bundle.ContinuousRiemannianMetric.metricChangeIsometry` (Topology/VectorBundle/Riemannian/MetricChange/Basic.lean) and `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameBundle.metricChangeHomeomorph` (OrthonormalFrame/MetricChange.lean). Missing: descent, smooth structures on lifts, and G4.3 onward. G4.1a–b frame adapters are done. The carriers the section names exist: `TauCeti.PrincipalBundle` with `PrincipalBundle.pullback`, cocycle gluing `TauCeti.fiberBundleCoreOfGroupCocycle`, `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameBundle` with its presentation as a principal `O(n)`-bundle, and `CliffordAlgebra.realCliffordSpinGroupZero`.
- Ready now: no for the rest: descent needs TopologicalKTheory T6.4's numerable principal-bundle category and CharacteristicClasses C1.0's oriented frames (neither in code). FourManifolds §4.1, the target for def 64, builds its Spin^c lifts on G4's frame lifts and covering adapters (FourManifolds README l.1695).

- Status: PARTIAL: the definition `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.HasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials` with `hasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials_iff`, the restriction bullet (`HasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials.of_isOpenImmersion`, `.restrict`) and the smooth-scheme bullet (`hasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials_of_smoothOfRelativeDimension`) are in Modules/Differentials/LocallyFreeQuotient/Basic.lean. The forward direction of the stalk criterion is in code: `HasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials.exists_surjective_stalk`, with the linear stalk maps `Scheme.Modules.Hom.stalkₗ`, `Hom.stalkₗ_surjective_of_epi` (AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/Stalk.lean) and the stalks of finite locally free sheaves `FiniteLocallyFreeSheaf.free_stalk`, `finrank_stalk` (AlgebraicGeometry/VectorBundle/Stalk.lean). The `X = ∅` and `d = 0` Checks are in code: `hasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials_of_isEmpty`, `hasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials_zero` (Modules/Differentials/LocallyFreeQuotient/Basic.lean, Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/LocallyFreeQuotientEmpty.lean). Also in code: the vanishing and comparison step of the uniqueness bullet on reduced schemes, `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.FiniteLocallyFreeSheaf.eq_zero_of_germ_eq_zero`, `hom_eq_zero_of_stalkₗ_eq_zero`, `existsUnique_comp_eq`, `existsUnique_iso_comp_eq` (AlgebraicGeometry/VectorBundle/Reduced.lean); at a point of `X_sm^{(d)}` the differentials `Ω[𝒪_{X,x}⁄k]` are free of rank `d`, `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.nonempty_linearEquiv_kaehlerDifferential_stalk_of_mem_smoothLocusOfDim`, `finrank_kaehlerDifferential_stalk_of_mem_smoothLocusOfDim` (Morphisms/Smooth/Locus/StalkDifferentials.lean); no quotient of rank above the relative dimension on a smooth scheme, `le_of_hasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials` with `HasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials.le_finrank_kaehlerDifferential_stalk` (Modules/Differentials/LocallyFreeQuotient/Basic.lean) and the `𝔸¹_k`, `d = 1, 2` Check (Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/LocallyFreeQuotient.lean); and the ring-level cusp Check in characteristic `2`: surjections from `Ω[A⁄k]` onto `A` share the kernel `A dx`, `TauCeti.CuspDifferentialsExample.ker_eq_span_D_cuspX`, `isUnit_map_D_cuspY` (Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/LocallyFreeQuotientCusp.lean); and the characteristic `≠ 2` half of the `k[ε]/(ε²)` Check, `TauCeti.LocallyFreeQuotientDualNumberExample.not_hasLocallyFreeQuotientOfDifferentials` (Examples/AlgebraicGeometry/Modules/LocallyFreeQuotientDualNumber.lean), on `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.StructureSheaf.toStalkAlgHom` (AlgebraicGeometry/StructureSheaf/Stalk/BaseAlgebra.lean). No STATUS.md (README last changed at roadmap 8c5685f0e, 2026-11-20; Tau Ceti checked at 553f7926aff, 2026-11-25). No target of the roadmap exists in Tau Ceti: grep `Stacks\.|IsMonomorphism|IsFiniteTypePoint|automorphismGroupSpace|IsGerbe|residualGerbe|ResidualGerbe|residueField.*Stack|normalSpace|tangentToNormal` in TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry empty. Every section rests on SchemeAndStackFoundations Layer 1, of which Tau Ceti has §1.3, the core of §1.4, §1.5's `EtaleEquivRel`, §1.18's `StackInGroupoids` carrier with the 2-fibre product `TauCeti.Stack.twoFiberProduct` (CategoryTheory/Bicategory/Functor/TwoFiberProduct.lean, stack property in CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/TwoFiberProduct) and stackification (`TauCeti.Stack.stackification`, CategoryTheory/Sites/Descent/Stackification/), §1.19's representability by algebraic spaces `TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.IsRepresentableBySpaces` (AlgebraicGeometry/Stack/Representable.lean) and §1.20's `StackInGroupoids.Hom.DVRLiftingAfterExtension` (Stack/ValuativeCriterion.lean); the rest of §1.19 (`IsAlgebraicStack`, `IsDeligneMumford`) and of §1.20 (`Stacks.points`, `inertia`, presentations) are not in code, so no item is ready. The README also names unowned "gap:" requests to its suppliers (most often gap:stack-morphism-permanence, gap:flat-groupoid-quotients, gap:stack-separation-axioms, gap:inertia-of-presentations, gap:reduced-regular-smooth-local, gap:qcoh-stacks-abelian-tensor; l.2069–l.2168 "What SchemeAndStackFoundations must provide (gap:...)"), which SchemeAndStackFoundations §§1.7–1.9, 1.13, 1.19–1.22, 4.12–4.13, AlgebraicModuliForArithmeticGeometry §8.1 and ReductiveGroups Layer 3 must add.

The lift is in code: `TauCeti.SpinCStructure`, a principal `Spinᶜ(n)`-bundle with a frame map to the orthonormal frame bundle, as an `OrthonormalFrameLift` along `spinCGroup.toSpecialOrthogonal`, with `toFrame_smul_mk`, `toFrame_smul_ofCircle` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/Basic.lean), its isomorphisms `OrthonormalFrameLift.Equiv` and pullbacks `OrthonormalFrameLift.pullback`, `Equiv.pullback` (Topology/VectorBundle/OrthonormalFrame/Lift/Basic.lean), and structures induced along a lift of the structure group, `OrthonormalFrameLift.extendSpinC` (which gives the Spin-induced and the unitary `Spinᶜ` structures). The determinant line is in code on `TauCeti.AssociatedBundle`: `TauCeti.SpinCStructure.determinantLine` (`P ×_{z²} ℂ`, with `finrank_determinantLine` and the weight-two central action `mk_ofCircle_smul_determinantLine`), with the rank-four half-spinor bundles `SpinCStructure.halfSpinorBundle`, Clifford multiplication `SpinCStructure.cliffordMul` and `exteriorPowerHalfSpinorEquiv` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/Spinor.lean, for SeibergWittenTheory §1.2). The structure-group part of boundary restriction is in code: `Spinᶜ(n)` for the standard form `CliffordAlgebra.realCliffordSpinCGroupZero` and the inclusion `CliffordAlgebra.realCliffordSpinCInclusion : Spinᶜ(n) → Spinᶜ(n + 1)` onto the stabilizer of the last coordinate vector, `range_realCliffordSpinCInclusion`, a closed embedding of topological groups (LinearAlgebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC/Inclusion.lean, Topology/Algebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC/Inclusion.lean). Compatibility with Spin structures is in code: `TauCeti.SpinStructure.toSpinC` with `inlSpinC` and the nowhere-vanishing section `determinantLineSection` of its determinant line (`determinantLineSection_ne_zero`, `continuous_determinantLineSection`, `determinantLineEquiv`, Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/OfSpin.lean). The bundle step of boundary restriction is in code: frames adapted to a unit section, `TauCeti.OrthonormalFrameBundle.sectionFrames`, locally trivial with fibre `O(N − 1)` (`exists_trivialization_sectionFrames`, Topology/VectorBundle/OrthonormalFrame/UnitSection.lean). Orientation reversal is in code: `TauCeti.SpinCStructure.reverse`, through `SpinCStructure.precompIsometry` along an isometry of the model and `OrthonormalFrameLift.precomp` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/Reflection.lean, OrthonormalFrame/Lift/Basic.lean), functorial and involutive, `TauCeti.SpinCStructure.Equiv.reverse`, `SpinCStructure.reverseReverseEquiv` (SpinStructure/Reflection/Equiv.lean). The first step of boundary restriction is in code: the points adapted to a unit section, `TauCeti.SpinCStructure.sectionPoints`, locally trivial with fibre `Spinᶜ(n)`, `SpinCStructure.exists_trivialization_sectionPoints`, on `TauCeti.PrincipalBundle.exists_trivialization_of_continuousOn_section_mem` (Topology/VectorBundle/SpinStructure/UnitSection.lean, Topology/FiberBundle/Principal/Reduction.lean). Locally trivial reductions of the frame bundle as `OrthonormalFrameLift`s are in code, `OrthonormalFrameBundle.reductionLift` (OrthonormalFrame/Reduction/Lift.lean), with the unitary frame lift `unitaryFrameLift` along `U(2) ⊆ SO(4)` of a rank-four bundle with a fibrewise orthogonal complex structure (OrthonormalFrame/ComplexStructure.lean); so is the group part of *sums*, `CliffordAlgebra.spinCGroupProd : Spinᶜ(Q₁) × Spinᶜ(Q₂) →* Spinᶜ(Q₁ ⊕ Q₂)` with `det_spinCGroupProd`, `toSpecialOrthogonal_spinCGroupProd`, `mem_ker_spinCGroupProd_iff` and continuity `spinCGroupProdContinuousMonoidHom` (LinearAlgebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC/Prod.lean, Topology/Algebra/CliffordAlgebra/Spin/SpinC/Prod.lean). ### §P.3 Hodge filtrations and reflex fields (the representation–Hodge dictionary)
- Status: PARTIAL: the direction from Hodge structures to representations of the Deligne torus is in code: `TauCeti.Hodge.HodgeStructureOn.complexDeligneAction`, `deligneAction`, `conj_deligneAction`, `complexDeligneAction_mu_apply_of_mem`, `complexDeligneAction_weight`, `deligneAction_neg_I`, `mem_piece_iff_complexDeligneAction_mu`, `eq_of_complexDeligneAction_mu`, `tateTwist_complexDeligneAction`, `dual_complexDeligneAction`, `tensorProduct_complexDeligneAction` (Geometry/Hodge/DeligneTorus.lean), on `TauCeti.DeligneTorus.S` with `complexSplitting`, `mu`, `characterGroup` (Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/DeligneTorus/{Basic,Splitting}.lean). The converse direction: the Hodge decomposition `TauCeti.DeligneTorus.hodgeComponent` of a real `S`-comodule, with `isInternal_hodgeComponent`, `map_conj_hodgeComponent` and `isHodgeDecomposition_hodgeComponent` for a comodule of weight `n` (Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/DeligneTorus/Hodge.lean, Coordinate.lean). The coordinate morphism `h : S → GL_{V,ℝ}` of a Hodge structure is in code: `TauCeti.Hodge.HodgeStructureOn.realCoordinateBialgHom` (in a basis fixed by the conjugation, by Galois descent of the complex coordinate morphism, with `realCoordinateBialgHom_genericMatrix`) and `HodgeStructureOn.deligneHom : ℝ ⊗[ℚ] O(GL_d) ⟶ O(S)` of a rational Hodge structure with a basis of `V_ℚ` (Geometry/Hodge/MumfordTate.lean). So is the field of definition `E(T, μ)` of a geometric cocharacter of a group of multiplicative type, the reflex field `E(T, h)` of a special pair, `TauCeti.MultiplicativeTypeCommHopfAlgCat.cocharacterFieldOfDefinition` with its Galois, functoriality and sum properties (Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/MultiplicativeType/{FieldOfDefinition,Cocharacter}.lean). On the comodule side, tensor and dual compatibility of `hodgeComponent` are in code (`DeligneTorus.hodgeComponent_tensor`, `DeligneTorus.mem_hodgeComponent_dual_iff`, with `DiagonalizableGroup.tmul_mem_weightSpace_tensor` and `DiagonalizableGroup.apply_eq_zero_of_mem_weightSpace_dual`, Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/DeligneTorus/Hodge/{TensorProduct,Dual}.lean), and so are the Tate comodules `ℝ(m)`, `DeligneTorus.tateComodule` on the norm character `normGroupLike`, of Hodge type `(-m, -m)` (`hodgeComponent_tateComodule`), and the elliptic Check on the tautological comodule `DeligneTorus.tautologicalComodule` (`hodgeComponent_tautologicalComodule_ne_bot_iff`) (Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/DeligneTorus/{Tate,Tautological}.lean), and the Tate-twist compatibility `DeligneTorus.hodgeComponent_tensor_tateComodule`, `(V ⊗ ℝ(m))^{p,q}` from `V^{p+m,q+m}` (Algebra/AlgebraicGroup/DeligneTorus/Hodge/TateTwist.lean); the round trip from Hodge structures is not.

### §1.8 Boundary parts, `W^{1,p}_Γ(Ω)` and Poincaré–Friedrichs (A8)
- Status: PARTIAL: admissible boundary parts are in code: `Set.IsAdmissibleBoundaryPart` with the charts `Set.IsBoundaryPartChart`, restriction, recentring, rigid-motion and dilation API (`isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_image_iff`, `isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_smul_iff`), `Set.IsLipschitzDomain.isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_empty`, `isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_frontier` and the half-space model `Set.isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_normalHalfSpace` (Analysis/Sobolev/LipschitzDomain/AdmissibleBoundaryPart.lean). So is `W^{1,p}_Γ(Ω)`, `TauCeti.w1pVanishingOnSubmodule` with `w1pVanishingOnSubmodule_antitone`, `w1pVanishingOnSubmodule_closure`, `w1p0Submodule_le_w1pVanishingOnSubmodule`, `w1pVanishingOnSubmodule_frontier` and `W1p.contDiffSMul_mem_w1pVanishingOnSubmodule` (Analysis/Sobolev/W1p/BoundaryPart.lean), with the empty case `w1pVanishingOnSubmodule_empty_of_isLipschitzDomainWith`, the zero trace on `Γ` `W1p.frontierTrace_ae_eq_zero_of_mem_w1pVanishingOnSubmodule` and Poincaré–Friedrichs `W1p.exists_norm_value_le_mul_norm_gradient_of_mem_w1pVanishingOnSubmodule` on a bounded preconnected Lipschitz domain when `μHE[dim E](Γ ∩ frontier Ω) ≠ 0` (Analysis/Sobolev/LipschitzDomain/BoundaryPart.lean). The Lipschitz shears are in code as the trace characterization uses them: the graph shear `TauCeti.graphFlattening` (Analysis/Calculus/GraphFlattening/Basic.lean) acts on `W^{1,p}` through `W1p.compMeasurePreservingL`, and the base shear transports the trace and its zero set, `W1p.halfSpaceTrace_compMeasurePreservingL_withLpMap_prodMap_id`, `W1p.halfSpaceTrace_compMeasurePreservingL_ae_eq_zero_iff` (Analysis/Sobolev/Trace/Tangential.lean). Missing: the trace characterization `W^{1,p}_Γ(Ω) = {u | T u = 0 on Γ}` for admissible `Γ`, and the rigid-motion and dilation invariance of `W^{1,p}_Γ`. Open Tau Ceti PRs https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4601 (invariance of `W^{1,p}_Γ` under changes of variables, rigid motions and dilations, `W1p.compL_mem_w1pVanishingOnSubmodule_iff`, `W1p.compAffineIsometryEquivL_mem_w1pVanishingOnSubmodule_iff`, Analysis/Sobolev/W1p/BoundaryPart/ChangeOfVariables.lean), https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4719 (the Lipschitz shears `normalShear` with Jacobian `1` and their mollifications, `lipschitzWith_normalShear`, `det_fderiv_normalShear`, `contDiff_normalShear_normed_convolution`) and https://git.taucetiproject.org/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pulls/4699 (the three square Checks, `isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_squareLeftPart_Ioo`, `not_isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_squareLeftPart_gaps`, `not_isAdmissibleBoundaryPart_squareLeftPart_punctured`).

Most of §8.6 item 1's finite Perron–Frobenius theorem is in code: the Perron root with a positive eigenvector, equal to the spectral radius and a simple root of the characteristic polynomial (`Matrix.IsIrreducible.exists_mulVec_eq_smul`, `IsIrreducible.rootMultiplicity_charpoly_eq_one`, LinearAlgebra/Matrix/PerronFrobenius/Irreducible.lean), and its strict dominance for primitive matrices (`Matrix.IsPrimitive.norm_lt_of_hasEigenvalue`, `IsPrimitive.norm_lt_of_mem_spectrum`, Primitive.lean). The convergence `B^n / ρ^n → v uᵀ / (u ⬝ᵥ v)` is not. The Birkhoff contraction that §8.6 consumes is in code (`TauCeti.Matrix.hilbertProjectiveDist_mulVec_le`, Data/Matrix/BirkhoffContraction.lean).
- Ready now: no. §8.4 needs §§0.3, 0.7, 4.2, 5.2–5.4, 7.1, 8.1–8.2. §8.5 needs §§3.3–3.4, 5.5, 6.4, 7.3, 8.1–8.2, 8.4. §8.6 needs §§0.7, 3.6, 4.1–4.3, 5.2, 5.4, 7.1, 8.1–8.4 and ProbabilisticAndMetricNumberTheoryPartII §3.3.
- Serves defs: 96
